# [1.0.0-beta.5](https://github.com/clagentic/clagentic-console/compare/v1.0.0-beta.4...v1.0.0-beta.5) (2026-05-13)

### Changes

* docs: full README rewrite with Clagentic:Console branding, C-arc logo, and correct philosophy
* docs: rewrite architecture.md, MODULE_MAP, MCP guide, ECOSYSTEM — remove all Clay/Mates references
* docs: add Clagentic logo assets (console-lockup, console-icon)
* chore: simplify PWA onboarding — remove Tailscale as blocking step, add push notifications skip
* chore: native-host rename clay-mcp-bridge → clagentic-mcp-bridge
* chore: LICENSE updated to include Clagentic:Console copyright
* chore: remove screenshot.gif, test-loop-output.txt, and Clay logo assets

### Reverts

* **main:** remove unrelated changes from [#330](https://github.com/clagentic/clagentic-console/issues/330) ([5eadb24](https://github.com/clagentic/clagentic-console/commit/5eadb24208a88cd612dff3e0f948b6a2ed205465))
* **ui:** remove icon strip DOM rebuild optimization and stale dot clearing ([c60a5a9](https://github.com/clagentic/clagentic-console/commit/c60a5a987590020643fcc974c0cbc38fa98ad323))


### BREAKING CHANGES

* Mate DM, mate creation, and all mate-related WS message
types are no longer handled. The add-user (+) button in the icon strip now
only appears in multi-user mode and adds human users only.

* **connection:** suppress overlay for brief disconnects, speed up reconnect ([27d0e82](https://github.com/clagentic/clagentic-console/commit/27d0e821f16fc71e39585b9ec1dfb8b7fa3f2f0e))

### BREAKING CHANGES

* Mate DM, mate creation, and all mate-related WS message
types are no longer handled. The add-user (+) button in the icon strip now
only appears in multi-user mode and adds human users only.
